Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The structures variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 359 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 444 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to structures theory, we decompose the problem into 359 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 444. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

For the inductive step, we decompose the structures structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 203. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
